## FILE: majid-pages.js
```
import { d as appState } from "./_astro/common.DNnWXwKG.js";
import { aJ as runtime } from "./_astro/index.xQnTCKcK.js";

const pages = {
  about: {
    title: "Majid Zafer, architect of tomorrow",
    body: `
      <p>Majid Zafer is a visionary architect of tomorrow, a bridge between ancient wisdom and cutting-edge possibility, thriving at the neon heartbeat of Las Vegas.</p>
      <p>Born in India and transplanted to America as a young dreamer, Zafer carries a relentless fire for equity, innovation, and impact. He is a devoted father, conscious capitalist, and relentless builder who transforms friction into momentum, turning bold ideas into scalable realities that honor both people and planet.</p>
      <p class="mz-kicker">[educate. empower. evolve.]</p>
    `,
  },
  dcrbn: {
    title: "DCRBN, zero to hero",
    body: `
      <p>As CEO and Co-Founder of DCRBN, Majid leads a Web3 venture studio and consulting force that catapults companies from zero to hero in the decentralized era.</p>
      <p>With over 15 years forging sustainability frontiers, he fuses blockchain, AI, quantum encryption, and operational intelligence into seamless strategies that deliver explosive 5x growth.</p>
      <p>DCRBN does not just consult. It engineers meteoric rises, crafting bespoke journeys where technology becomes a force for good.</p>
    `,
  },
  speaking: {
    title: "Speaker, strategist, advisor",
    body: `
      <p>A dynamic public speaker, brand strategist, and advisor, Majid moves effortlessly across ecosystems, partnering with trailblazers like Cory Warfield, amplifying platforms like Phyll, and igniting rooms at Las Vegas expos, NFL Alumni events, and beyond.</p>
      <p>Whether demystifying Web3 for enterprise leaders or championing local businesses in the desert city he calls home, his presence commands attention and inspires action.</p>
    `,
  },
  legacy: {
    title: "The Zafer legacy",
    body: `
      <p>In a world racing toward decentralization, Majid Zafer stands as the calm, magnetic strategist who removes friction between groundbreaking tech and human potential.</p>
      <p>Educate. Empower. Evolve. That is the Zafer legacy: building brands, futures, and movements that do not just scale. They illuminate.</p>
      <p class="mz-kicker">[brands. futures. movements. illuminated.]</p>
    `,
  },
};

const routeOrder = ["about", "dcrbn", "speaking", "legacy"];

function routeFromHash() {
  const route = window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, "");
  return pages[route] ? route : "home";
}

function setRoute(route, push = true) {
  const normalized = pages[route] ? route : "home";
  if (push) {
    const hash = normalized === "home" ? "#home" : `#${normalized}`;
    if (window.location.hash !== hash) history.pushState(null, "", hash);
  }
  renderRoute(normalized);
}

function openSidebar() {
  if (!runtime.size) {
    window.setTimeout(openSidebar, 100);
    return;
  }
  appState.isSidebarActive.value = true;
}

function closeSidebar() {
  appState.isSidebarActive.value = false;
}

function renderRoute(route) {
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-mz-route]").forEach((link) => {
    link.classList.toggle("is-active", link.dataset.mzRoute === route);
  });

  if (route === "home") {
    closeSidebar();
    return;
  }

  const page = pages[route] || pages.about;
  const title = document.querySelector(".js-mz-page-title");
  const body = document.querySelector(".js-mz-page-body");
  if (title) title.textContent = page.title;
  if (body) body.innerHTML = page.body;
  openSidebar();
}

function wireNavigation() {
  document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
    const routeLink = event.target.closest("[data-mz-route]");
    if (!routeLink) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    setRoute(routeLink.dataset.mzRoute);
  });

  document.querySelector(".js-learn")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (routeFromHash() === "home") setRoute("about");
    else renderRoute(routeFromHash());
  });

  window.addEventListener("hashchange", () => renderRoute(routeFromHash()));
  window.addEventListener("popstate", () => renderRoute(routeFromHash()));
  renderRoute(routeFromHash());
}

function boot() {
  const sidebar = document.querySelector(".js-sidebar");
  if (!sidebar) return;

  sidebar.innerHTML = `
    <nav class="mz-nav" aria-label="Majid Zafer sections">
      <a href="#home" data-mz-route="home">Home</a>
      ${routeOrder.map((route) => `<a href="#${route}" data-mz-route="${route}">${route}</a>`).join("")}
    </nav>
    <div class="font-bold uppercase mb-5 mz-page-title js-mz-page-title" data-astro-cid-edwrpnhe></div>
    <div class="mz-page-body js-mz-page-body" data-astro-cid-edwrpnhe></div>
    <div class="w-full h-[1px] bg-white my-20 opacity-50" data-astro-cid-edwrpnhe></div>
    <a href="#home" class="mz-home-link" data-mz-route="home" data-astro-cid-edwrpnhe>Back home</a>
    <div class="font-bold pointer-events-auto cursor-pointer js-sidebar-close" data-astro-cid-edwrpnhe>CLOSE</div>
  `;

  document.querySelector(".js-sidebar-close")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    closeSidebar();
    if (window.location.hash && window.location.hash !== "#home") history.pushState(null, "", "#home");
  });

  document.querySelector('a[href="https://majidzafer.is/"]')?.setAttribute("href", "#home");
  document.querySelector('a[href="#home"]')?.removeAttribute("target");
  wireNavigation();
}

if (document.readyState === "loading") {
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
} else {
  boot();
}
```

```
.mz-nav {

  content: "[";
}

  content: "]";

.mz-nav a:hover {
  text-decoration: underline;
}

  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.mz-page-body p {
  margin-bottom: 2rem;
}

.mz-page-body .mz-kicker {
  font-weight: 700;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.mz-home-link {
  margin-top: 2rem;
  display: inline-block;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

.mz-home-link:hover {
  text-decoration: underline;
}
```
